Motivation: 玉米果穗几何性状（长度、宽度、曲率和体积）与产量及籽粒灌浆密切相关，但现有高通量表型管线受限于成本高、劳动强度大和需要专用硬件的瓶颈，亟需一种低成本、低人工干预的3D重建方案。

Method: 使用消费级DSLR在电动转盘和均匀LED光照下拍摄单段20秒视频；采用多视角COLMAP获取相机位姿并初始化NeRF网络，重建水密3D网格；视频中每帧可见的已知直径圆柱支架提供自动公制尺度标定与下游几何质量控制；管线自动处理全部下游流程，仅需极少量人工操作。

Result: 对300个来自多样性玉米自交系群体的果穗进行测试，250个（83.3%）通过自动处理与质控。骨架长度与卡尺测量R²=0.964（RMSE=4.68 mm）；凸包体积与水置换法在15个穗子子集上R²=0.982（RMSE=5.26 mL）。长度残差随曲率增大而增大，但包围盒高度（与卡尺同为直线弦长）无此趋势，表明差异源于测量定义而非误差。硬件成本约607美元，人工操作从约5分钟降至1分钟，下游处理全自动。

Conclusion: 该平台实现了育种规模下低成本、自动化、高通量的玉米果穗3D表型分析，为3D果穗表型测量奠定了基础。测量偏差的原因是卡尺记录弦长而骨架长度追踪测地弧长，可通过统一测量定义来消除。

Abstract: Maize ear geometry (length, width, curvature, and volume) is closely tied to yield and grain-filling outcomes, but existing high-throughput phenotyping pipelines remain constrained by the cost, labor, and specialized hardware they require. We developed and validated a low-cost pipeline that reconstructs a watertight 3-D mesh of a maize ear from a single 20-second video captured with a consumer-grade DSLR on a motorized turntable under uniform LED illumination. Camera poses from a multi-seed COLMAP procedure initialize a Neural Radiance Field (NeRF), and a cylindrical holder of known diameter, visible in every frame, provides automatic metric scaling with downstream geometric quality control. Applied to 300 ears spanning a diverse maize inbred panel, 250 (83.3%) passed automated processing and quality control. Skeleton length agreed with manual caliper measurements across all 250 ears (R^2 = 0.964, RMSE = 4.68 mm), and convex-hull volume agreed with water-displacement volume on a 15-ear subset spanning the full size range (R^2 = 0.982, RMSE = 5.26 mL). Residual length error grew with ear curvature, whereas bounding-box height, which records the same straight-line chord as calipers, showed no such trend; the discrepancy therefore originates in the measurement definition, since calipers record the chord while skeleton length traces the geodesic arc. The capture hardware costs approximately 607 USD, and operator involvement fell from roughly five minutes to one minute per ear, with all downstream processing running unattended. The platform provides a foundation for breeding-scale 3-D ear phenotyping.

</details>


### [170] [ATGS: Anchored Temporal Gaussian Splatting for Long Volumetric Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30184)
*Jiahao Wu,Jie Liang,Die Hu,Jiayu Yang,Kaiqiang Xiong,Xiang Li,Xiaoyun Zheng,Chao Wang,Ronggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ATGS，一种基于高斯泼溅的体视频重建框架，通过时间条件锚点组织高斯原语并采用时间窗口策略，解决长序列复杂运动下的时间不稳定性和视觉伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体视频方法在处理长序列和复杂运动时表现不佳，常出现时间不稳定性和视觉伪影。作者的关键洞察是直接用单个高斯原语显式追踪长期复杂运动本质上是不可靠的，因此需要改变组织高斯的方式以降低运动复杂度。

Method: 1) 将高斯原语组织在时间条件锚点周围，锚点定位于其空间和时间支持域，降低长程运动复杂度；2) 引入时间窗口化策略，仅激活与查询时间相关的锚点，提升可扩展性和时间连贯性；3) 设计紧凑的多层级锚点特征集合，编码全局特征、局部空间特征和局部时间特征，共同约束高斯生成。

Result: 广泛实验表明，ATGS在具有复杂运动的长序列体视频上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过锚点组织和时间窗口机制，该框架有效提升了长时间体视频重建的时间和空间稳定性，为复杂动态场景的高质量体视频重建提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Volumetric video enables immersive free viewpoint rendering of dynamic real world scenes, yet existing methods struggle with long sequences and complex motions, often leading to temporal instability and visual artifacts. To address these challenges, we propose \ourname, a Gaussian splatting based framework for volumetric video reconstruction. Our key insight is that explicitly tracking long term complex motion with individual Gaussian primitives is inherently unstable. Instead, we organize Gaussians around time conditioned anchors that localize their spatial and temporal support, thereby reducing long range motion complexity. We further introduce a temporal windowing strategy to activate only anchors relevant to the queried time, which improves scalability and temporal coherence. In addition, to ensure spatial and temporal stability, we design a compact set of multi level anchor features that encode global features, local spatial features, and local temporal features, jointly constraining Gaussian generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \ourname \ consistently outperforms prior methods on long sequence volumetric videos with complex motions. Project page: https://github.com/WuJH2001/ATGS.

</details>


### [171] [NoisEasier: Test-Time Noise Optimization for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30194)
*Yujiang Pu,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NoisEasier，一种测试时扩展框架，通过可微奖励引导的噪声优化提升文本到视频生成的细粒度组合对齐能力，无需修改底层模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在文本到视频（T2V）生成中仍难以实现细粒度的组合对齐（如属性绑定、空间关系、对象交互）。现有奖励微调方法易受奖励黑客攻击且对新的提示分布适应性差。

Method: NoisEasier通过可微奖励引导的噪声优化实现测试时扩展，结合高效短步生成器与多目标奖励公式，联合优化整个随机轨迹而非仅初始潜在变量，在现实推理预算下实现稳定且实用的测试时优化。

Result: 在VBench和T2V-CompBench上的实验表明，在多个骨干网络上取得一致性改进，在属性绑定、对象交互和计数等具有挑战性的维度上平均提升超过10%。

Conclusion: NoisEasier既可作为奖励微调的灵活替代方案，也可作为其补充增强，确立了测试时扩展作为可控文本到视频生成的有效范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently advanced text-to-video (T2V) generation, yet they still struggle with fine-grained compositional alignment, such as attribute binding, spatial relations, and object interactions. While reward-based fine-tuning improves alignment, it is susceptible to reward hacking and adapts poorly to new prompt distributions. In this work, we propose NoisEasier, a test-time scaling framework that improves T2V generation through differentiable reward-guided noise optimization without modifying the underlying model. By combining efficient short-step generators with a multi-objective reward formulation, NoisEasier enables stable and practical test-time optimization under realistic inference budgets. Our key insight is that jointly optimizing the entire stochastic trajectory accelerates reward convergence and improves compositional alignment over optimizing only the initial latent, with negligible additional computational and time cost. Experiments on VBench and T2V-CompBench demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple backbones, achieving over 10% average gains on challenging dimensions such as attribute binding, object interaction, and numeracy. Overall, NoisEasier serves as both a flexible alternative and a complementary enhancement to reward-based fine-tuning, establishing test-time scaling as an effective paradigm for controllable text-to-video generation.

</details>


### [172] [DICS: Exploring Data Intrinsic Consistency for Visual Instruction Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30209)
*Yuyang Hong,Jinhui Guo,Jiaqi Gu,Lubin Fan,Ruixiang Wang,Kun Ding,Yue Wu,Shiming Xiang,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出数据内在一致性（DIC）度量与自适应数据选择方法（DICS），用于在多模态指令数据中筛选高质量子集，在仅用25%数据的情况下超越全量微调性能，并构建了6M规模的DICS-6M语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉指令微调对提升VLM能力至关重要，但现有数据选择方法多依赖分布多样性或启发式过滤，忽视单个样本内部组件之间的一致性。固定比例约束下如何从快速扩展的数据集中选择最优子集仍是瓶颈。

Method: 提出DIC自评分度量，包含两个模块：视觉信息一致性（VIC）评估视觉内容与指令之间的对齐程度，以及响应信息一致性（RIC）评估响应对指令的连贯性。基于DIC进一步提出DICS自适应数据选择方法，在不同数据预算下优化样本内高一致性与全局分布多样性之间的权衡。

Result: 在多种数据集规模和模型架构上，DICS一致优于现有最先进方法；仅用LLaVA-1.5-665K的25%数据即超越全量微调性能。在DICS-6M上，用不到官方训练数据的25%达到了InternVL3-8B-Instruct官方性能的94.52%。

Conclusion: 样本级内在一致性是数据选择的强有力指标，将内部一致性与全局多样性相结合的自适应选择策略可显著提升数据效率和模型性能，且具有良好的跨架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual instruction tuning is crucial for advancing the vision-language alignment and instruction-following capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, identifying optimal subsets under a fixed ratio constraint from rapidly expanding datasets remains a significant bottleneck. While existing methods largely depend on distribution diversity or heuristic filtering, they often overlook the internal coherence within individual samples. To bridge this gap, we propose Data Intrinsic Consistency (DIC), a self-scoring metric designed to quantify the sample-level inter-component consistency. DIC consists of two modules: Visual Information Consistency (VIC), evaluating the alignment between visual content and instructions, and Response Information Consistency (RIC), assessing response coherence relative to the instruction. Building upon DIC, we introduce Data Intrinsic Consistency Selection (DICS), an adaptive data selection method that optimizes the trade-off between high intra-sample consistency and global distributional diversity under varying data budgets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse dataset scales and model architectures, surpassing full-dataset fine-tuning while using only 25% of the LLaVA-1.5-665K data. We further curate DICS-6M, a 6M-sample multi-modal instruction corpus that enables the largest-scale visual instruction selection study to date; remarkably, DICS reaches 94.52\% of the official InternVL3-8B-Instruct performance using less than 25\% of its reported training data. Code can be seen at https://github.com/cqu-student/DICS

</details>


### [173] [Towards a Joint Khmer Text Recognition and Word Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30213)
*Marry Kong,Rina Buoy,Sovisal Chenda,Nguonly Taing,Masakazu Iwamura,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的高棉语文本识别与词分割联合框架，通过CTC解码器实现并行快速解码，可同时完成字符识别和词边界定位，取代传统的先识别后分割两阶段流水线，在多种文档模态（印刷、场景、手写）基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语不使用可见词分隔符，传统的方式需要先进行文本识别，再额外执行词分割步骤，这需要两个独立的顺序模型，不仅容易累积错误，还会为大规模文档处理引入显著的延迟。因此有必要将两个任务统一到单一模型中，以减少错误传播和推理延迟。

Method: 提出一个联合高棉语文本识别与词分割的统一模型，采用连接主义时间分类（CTC）解码器实现快速并行解码。模型可通过参数b进行控制：设定b=1时输出带词边界的词分割结果，b=0时仅进行纯字符识别，从而在一个模型中灵活支持两种模式。

Result: 在覆盖不同文档模态（印刷文档、场景图像、手写图像）的多个基准数据集上，实验表明该模型不仅能识别文档图像中的字符，还能准确定位词边界，消除了传统顺序流水线中额外词分割步骤的需求。

Conclusion: 该联合框架能够在保持CTC快速并行解码优势的同时，将文本识别与词分割统一到一个模型中，有效减少了大型文档处理中的延迟和错误传播，为高棉语等无词分隔符语言的文档处理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Text recognition, or extracting electronic text from document images, has been indispensable for knowledge retrieval tasks, such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). For Khmer, extracted text is subject to an extra word segmentation step, as Khmer does not use any visible word delimiters to denote word boundaries. Thus, a recognition-then-segmentation pipeline for Khmer requires two separate sequential models; this is not only error-prone but also adds significant latency for large-scale document processing. This paper proposes a novel joint Khmer text recognition and word segmentation framework in a unified model. The proposed model, using a connectionist-temporal-classification (CTC) decoder for fast, parallel decoding, can be instructed to recognize Khmer text with ($b=1$) and without ($b=0$) word segmentation. Experimental results on different benchmark datasets of different document modalities (document, scene, and handwritten images) show that the proposed model can not only recognize characters in document images but also locate word boundaries, removing the need for an extra word segmentation step in a conventional sequential pipeline.

</details>


### [174] [Amortized Anchor Refinement for Deployable Continuous-Time 4D Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30218)
*Jingong Chen,Qingwen Zhang,Sanghyeon Jun,Chulwoo Pack,Kyle Gao,Kwanghee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了摊销锚点细化（Amortized Anchor Refinement）方法，利用冻结的骨干网络预测初始高斯表示，并配合固定计算预算下的短期优化来专门化场景，同时通过容量下限保留表示密度。在部署阶段，使用训练无关的持久同调约束来修剪不稳定的高斯体，同时保留拓扑持久结构，并将轨迹直接流式化为场景流。实验表明该方法在Stage-Capture基准上达到24.31±2.22dB，可在单块消费级GPU上完成重建，并在独立XR头显上实现回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间4D重建在独立XR头显上难以实际应用：逐场景优化需要部署端不可行的计算量，且较低计算预算会导致重建彻底崩溃而非优雅退化；前馈预测虽快但难以恢复场景特定细节。因此需要一种在固定计算预算下兼顾速度与精细度的4D重建方法。

Method: 使用冻结骨干网络预测初始高斯表示（前馈阶段），随后在固定计算预算下进行短期优化以专门化场景表示；引入容量下限（capacity floor）防止表示密度丢失。训练无关阶段采用持久同调（persistent homology）约束，修剪不稳定的高斯体同时保留拓扑持久结构，并将修剪后的轨迹直接流式化为场景流输出。

Result: 在Stage-Capture基准上达到24.31±2.22dB的PSNR；部署实验表明可在单块消费级GPU上于目标预算内完成重建，并能在独立XR头显上实时回放。

Conclusion: 摊销锚点细化通过结合前馈预测与受限优化的混合策略，弥补了前馈方法细节不足与逐场景优化计算过高的缺陷，配合持久同调剪枝在保持拓扑结构的同时压缩表示，实现了在XR头显等边缘设备上的可行连续时间4D重建。

Abstract: Continuous-time 4D reconstruction remains impractical on standalone XR headsets. Per-scene optimization demands deployment-infeasible compute, and lower budgets cause collapse rather than degrade gradually. Feed-forward prediction is fast, but struggle to recover scene-specific detail. We present Amortized Anchor Refinement, which uses a frozen backbone to predict an initial Gaussian representation and a short optimization to specialize it under a fixed compute budget, with a capacity floor preserving representational density. A training-free stage then applies a persistent-homology constraint to prune unstable Gaussians while preserving topologically persistent structures, and streams the resulting trajectories directly as scene flow. On the Stage-Capture benchmark, Amortized Anchor Refinement achieves 24.31$\pm$2.22dB, while our deployment experiments demonstrate reconstruction within the target budget on a single consumer GPU and playback on a standalone XR headset.

</details>


### [175] [Semantic-Spatial Discriminability Enhancement for Generalized Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30233)
*Kaiyan Lei,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义-空间判别性增强框架（SSDE），用于广义视觉定位任务，通过语义判别性增强模块（SeDE）和空间判别性增强模块（SpDE）分别提升细粒度语义理解和实例级空间定位能力，在十个数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义视觉定位（GVG）任务在复杂多目标场景中，现有方法依赖全局语义匹配或粗粒度区域交互，容易混淆视觉相似的目标，难以建立稳定的实例级决策边界，缺乏细粒度语义和空间判别性。

Method: 提出SSDE框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 语义判别性增强模块（SeDE），利用空间引导的交叉注意力解耦目标相关的细粒度视觉属性，并与文本主体语义融合；(2) 空间判别性增强模块（SpDE），建模实例中心密度图以刻画目标空间分布，并作为辅助监督信号显式构建实例分离结构。

Result: 在十个数据集上，SSDE在经典视觉定位和广义视觉定位任务中均取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: SSDE通过同时增强语义和空间两个维度的判别性，有效解决了广义视觉定位中多目标场景下的实例混淆问题，为跨模态理解和实例级定位提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Generalized Visual Grounding (GVG) task aims to localize targets in an image based on referring expressions, extends the classical visual grounding paradigm by integrating multi-target and non-target scenarios. Previous methods typically rely on global semantic matching or coarse-grained region interactions for localization, where the discriminative cues are primarily derived from sentence-level semantics or regional context. In complex multi-target scenarios, such approaches tend to confuse visually similar targets, making it difficult to establish stable instance-level decision boundaries. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel Semantic-Spatial Discriminability Enhancement (SSDE) framework for generalized visual grounding, which aims to enhance the discriminative ability on fine-grained semantics and spatial localization, improving both cross-modal understanding and instance-level grounding. Specifically, to enhance the semantic discriminability of query representations at the fine-grained level, we propose a Semantic Discriminability Enhancement (SeDE) module, which leverages spatially guided cross-attention to disentangle fine-grained target-relevant visual attributes and integrates them with the textual subject semantics. Furthermore, to strengthen the spatial discriminability of the referred targets, we introduce a Spatial Discriminability Enhancement (SpDE) module, which models an instance center density map to characterize the spatial distribution of targets, and explicitly constructs instance separation structures in the spatial domain by employing them as an auxiliary supervision signal. Extensive experiments show that SSDE achieves superior performance on ten datasets across both classic and generalized visual grounding tasks.

</details>


### [176] [OPUS: A Simple yet Effective Unified Framework for Open-Vocabulary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30247)
*Xiaoyan Wei,Zhimin Yao,Ruilin Yang,Wei Zhang,Yong Dai,Yi Zhang,Wei Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPUS是一个统一的开放词汇检测器，通过语义丰富的视觉表示和可扩展的grounding监督，在不依赖复杂跨模态融合、分阶段训练或迭代标注的情况下，同时支持文本查询、视觉示例、泛化视觉和混合提示，在COCO、LVIS-minival和ODinW35上达到SOTA性能（Visual-I指标68.1/69.2/54.7 AP），并实现了混合提示的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一开放词汇检测（OVD）方法虽然支持多种异构提示（文本查询、视觉示例及其组合），但往往依赖日益复杂的系统设计，如重型跨模态融合、分阶段训练和迭代标注管线。作者重新审视在更强基础模型时代，这些复杂性是否仍然必要，旨在探索是否可以用更简单的架构实现同样甚至更强的统一提示能力。

Method: OPUS采用简单的三部分设计：（1）模型架构将语义丰富的视觉编码器（基于DINOv3-ConvNeXt-B骨干网络，配备高效混合编码）与提示感知解码器相结合，避免为不同提示类型设置专用分支，实现统一提示推理；（2）采用单阶段文本-视觉联合训练策略，引入实例级对比对齐（ICA）；（3）基于SAM3的单遍数据引擎，为异构grounding监督生成训练数据。通过这三部分设计，OPUS避免了传统复杂管线，实现了简洁的统一框架。

Result: 在COCO、LVIS-minival和ODinW35基准上的实验表明，OPUS在Visual-I指标上达到SOTA性能（68.1/69.2/54.7 AP），同时保持均衡的Text和Visual-G准确率。此外，OPUS成功将混合提示从干扰转化为互补，能够超越单独使用文本或视觉提示的效果。

Conclusion: OPUS证明了简单性与强大的统一提示能力可以同时实现。通过利用更强基础模型带来的语义丰富视觉表示，以及可扩展的grounding监督，无需复杂设计即可实现先进的统一开放词汇检测性能，并验证了混合提示可以产生互补增益而非相互干扰。

Abstract: Recent unified open-vocabulary detection (OVD) supports heterogeneous prompts, including text queries, visual exemplars, and their combinations, but often rely on increasingly complex designs such as heavy cross-modal fusion, staged training, and iterative annotation pipelines. We revisit whether such complexity is necessary in the era of stronger foundation models. Our finding is that unified OVD can be made substantially simpler with semantic-rich visual representations and scalable grounding supervision. We present OPUS (\textbf{O}pen-vocabulary, \textbf{P}rompt-\textbf{U}nified, \textbf{S}imple), a unified detector supporting text, interactive visual, generic visual, and mixed prompting within one framework. OPUS adopts a simple three-part design. Its model architecture combines a semantic-rich visual encoder, built on a DINOv3-ConvNeXt-B backbone with efficient hybrid encoding, with a prompt-aware decoder that avoids prompt-specific branches for unified prompt reasoning. OPUS is trained with a one-stage text-visual training strategy with Instance-level Contrastive Alignment (ICA), and is supported by a SAM3-based single-pass data engine for heterogeneous grounding supervision. Experiments on COCO, LVIS-minival, and ODinW35 show that OPUS achieves state-of-the-art Visual-I performance, reaching 68.1/69.2/54.7 AP, while maintaining balanced Text and Visual-G accuracy. OPUS also turns mixed prompting from interference into complementarity, improving over text or visual prompt alone. These results show that simplicity and strong unified prompting capability can be achieved together.

</details>


### [177] [Centering before Pruning: Lightweight Geometry Correction for Diversity-Based Visual Token Pruning in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30263)
*Shunjie Wen,Jaeyeon Lee,Dong-Wan Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cen-Prune方法，通过中心化余弦相似度结合原始空间显著性偏好，改进基于多样性的视觉令牌剪枝，以降低大视觉语言模型的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）因冗长且高度冗余的视觉令牌序列而产生高昂推理成本。基于多样性的剪枝通过成对余弦相似度选择令牌子集来缓解这一成本，但原始视觉令牌之间的相似度高度集中在正区间，难以区分非冗余令牌。中心化特征虽能揭示更丰富的成对结构，但单独使用时反而降低剪枝性能，需要揭示这一矛盾背后的原因并提出改进方案。

Method: 本文首先通过分析发现中心化虽能更好地解析子集多样性，但失去了原始空间中隐含的令牌级显著性偏好（即对全局独特令牌的偏好，这些令牌通常包含语义信息丰富的内容）。基于此，提出Cen-Prune方法，使用中心化余弦相似度衡量子集多样性，同时保留原始空间的显著性和令牌级偏好作为互补信号。该方法为轻量级、即插即用的修正方案，不改变底层选择机制，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在多个图像和视频理解基准以及多种LVLM架构上的大量实验表明，Cen-Prune在现有基于多样性的剪枝方法基础上提供了稳健的性能改进。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了多样性（diversity）与显著性（distinctiveness）在原始几何空间中相互纠缠的关系，并证明通过中心化余弦相似度与原始空间显著性偏好的结合，可以有效提升基于多样性剪枝的性能，为降低LVLM推理成本提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) incur substantial inference costs due to their long and highly redundant visual-token sequences. Diversity-based pruning mitigates this cost by selecting token subsets based on pairwise cosine similarity. We find, however, that similarities between raw visual tokens are strongly concentrated in the positive range, limiting their ability to distinguish non-redundant tokens. A natural way to improve this resolution is to center token features before computing cosine similarity. Centering indeed reveals a substantially richer pairwise structure, yet unexpectedly degrades pruning performance when used alone. We show that this apparent contradiction arises because the raw geometry does more than represent pairwise diversity: it also implicitly favors globally distinctive tokens, which tend to contain semantically informative content. Centering better resolves subset diversity but loses this useful token-wise preference, revealing that diversity and distinctiveness are entangled in the raw geometry. Based on this analysis, we propose the \textbf{Cen}tered Geometry \textbf{Prune}r (Cen-Prune), which measures subset diversity using centered cosine similarity while retaining raw-space distinctiveness as a complementary token-wise preference. This lightweight, plug-and-play correction leaves the underlying selection mechanism unchanged and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple image- and video-understanding benchmarks and LVLM architectures demonstrate that Cen-Prune provides robust improvements in overall performance across existing diversity-based pruners.

</details>


### [178] [Motion-Saliency Complementary Masked Modeling for Point Cloud Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30279)
*Wei Wang,Yiding Sun,Yuyan Wang,Zhuoyue Zhang,Zhengqiao Li,Dongfu Yin,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoSaiC框架，一种基于运动显著性互补掩码建模的自监督点云视频表示学习方法，通过课程运动显著性掩码、正规流运动建模和跨视角令牌一致性预测三个组件联合捕捉外观与运动动态，在多项下游任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云视频表示学习对3D动态场景理解至关重要，但现有自监督方法未能充分挖掘运动信息与外观信息之间的互补关系，需要更有效的框架来同时捕捉外观和运动动态。

Method: MoSaiC包含三个核心组件：(1) Curriculum Motion-Saliency Masking (CMSM)——在课程调度下引导掩码过程聚焦于运动显著性令牌；(2) Normal-Flow Motion (NFM) modeling——在李代数so(3)中监督每个令牌的局部刚体旋转作为显式几何运动目标；(3) Cross-view Token Consistency Prediction (CTCP)——在两个互补掩码视图间强制执行令牌级一致性。

Result: 在动作识别、时间动作分割和点级语义分割等多个下游任务上的广泛实验证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MoSaiC通过将运动显著性掩码、几何运动建模和跨视图一致性相结合，能够有效捕捉点云视频中的外观与运动动态，为自监督点云视频表示学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Point cloud video representation learning is crucial for 3D dynamic scene understanding. In this paper, we propose MoSaiC, a novel Motion-Saliency Complementary masked modeling framework for self-supervised point cloud video representation learning. MoSaiC couples three components: Curriculum Motion-Saliency Masking (CMSM), which guides the masking process toward motion-salient tokens under a curriculum schedule; Normal-Flow Motion (NFM) modeling, which supervises the local rigid rotation of each token in the Lie algebra so(3) as an explicit geometric motion target; and Cross-view Token Consistency Prediction (CTCP), which enforces consistency between two complementary masked views at the token level. Together, these components allow MoSaiC to effectively capture both appearance and motion dynamics. Extensive experiments on multiple downstream tasks, including action recognition, temporal action segmentation, and point-level semantic segmentation, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [179] [SELECT: SELEctive Context Transfer for Class-Incremental Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30281)
*Avi Gupta,Saurabh Yadav,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Tammam Tillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SELECT提出了一种选择性上下文迁移方法来解决类增量语义分割中的灾难性遗忘和背景偏移问题，通过将每个新类仅与少量语义相似的历史类关联，并利用上下文迁移注意力机制构建结构化初始化，在Pascal VOC和ADE20K上分别取得2.2%和2.8%的mIoU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CISS方法在应对稳定性-可塑性困境时，往往采用无差别的知识迁移或模糊的初始化策略，这会稀释重要的语义信息，无法有效利用先验知识。

Method: 提出了SELECT框架，核心包括：(1) 选择性上下文迁移，将每个新类别与一组语义相似的历史类别关联；(2) 上下文迁移注意力机制(Context Transfer Attention)，从相似类中聚合学习到的token为新类构建结构化初始化；(3) 受控噪声扰动和基于边界的上下文迁移损失，确保新类token与其源token保持分离，防止迁移破坏借用的表示。

Result: 在Pascal VOC和ADE20K上进行大量实验，SELECT一致优于现有方法，mIoU分别提升2.2%（VOC）和2.8%（ADE）。

Conclusion: SELECT通过选择性上下文迁移有效缓解了稳定性-可塑性困境，为CISS中的知识迁移提供了精准而非盲目的处理方式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Semantic Segmentation (CISS) is fundamentally challenged by catastrophic forgetting and background shift, where learning new concepts degrades performance on previously seen classes. While existing methods attempt to balance stability (retaining old knowledge) and plasticity (learning new knowledge), they often fail to leverage prior knowledge effectively. These approaches typically rely on indiscriminate knowledge transfer or ambiguous initializations, which can dilute crucial semantic information. To overcome this limitation, we propose SELECT, a novel approach for Selective Context Transfer, which instead grounds each new class in a small set of semantically similar past classes. Its core is a Context Transfer Attention mechanism that aggregates the learned tokens from similar classes into a structured initialization for the new class. To ensure this transfer does not corrupt the borrowed representations, we add a controlled noise perturbation and a margin-based context-transfer loss that enforces separation between the new class token and its source tokens. Extensive experiments on Pascal VOC and ADE20K show that SELECT consistently outperforms prior work, achieving mIoU of 2.2% on VOC and 2.8% on ADE, providing an effective handle on the stability-plasticity dilemma. Code is available at https://github.com/avigupta2798/SELECT.

</details>


### [180] [Dynamic Hub-and-Spoke Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30294)
*Xinru Jiang,Lin Zhao,Xi Xiao,Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Chenrui Ma,Haolin Li,Yanzhi Wang,Yifan Gong,Octavia Camps*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的Dynamic Hub-and-Spoke Memory (D-HSM)框架，用于流式视频理解。该方法将远处历史视频片段转换为结构化文本记忆，以实体为中心组织为枢纽-辐射状记忆结构，仅在回答问题时动态检索与问题相关的紧凑记忆子集，并结合近期视觉窗口进行答案预测，在流式和长视频基准上均显著提升VLM骨干网络的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要在持续增长的视觉流中任意时刻回答问题，核心挑战在于如何紧凑地记忆远距离历史信息，同时有效检索与问题相关的证据。现有方法难以在长程历史记忆与细粒度近期感知之间取得平衡。

Method: D-HSM是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架。它将选定的历史视频片段转换为类型化的文本观测，并存储在以实体为中心的枢纽-辐射（hub-and-spoke）记忆中，其中实体作为枢纽（hub），相关证据作为辐射（spoke）。回答问题时，动态检索紧凑的问题感知记忆子集，通过枢纽-辐射链接进行扩展，再与近期视觉窗口结合，输入冻结的VLM进行答案预测。

Result: 在流式视频和长视频基准上的大量实验表明，D-HSM一致且显著地提升了VLM骨干网络的性能，并超越了其他最先进的在线和离线视频理解基线。

Conclusion: D-HSM通过结构化文本记忆与问题感知动态检索的结合，有效解决了流式视频理解中长程记忆保持与证据检索的核心难题，且无需额外训练，具有较强的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires answering questions at arbitrary times over a continuously growing visual stream. The central challenge is to compactly remember long-range history while effectively retrieving question-relevant evidence. We propose Dynamic Hub-and-Spoke Memory (D-HSM), a training-free framework that represents distant history as structured textual memory while preserving the recent frames as visual tokens for fine-grained perception. Specifically, D-HSM turns selected historical video chunks into typed textual observations and stores them in an entity-centered hub-and-spoke memory, with entities as hubs and related evidence as spokes. When answering a question, D-HSM dynamically retrieves a compact question-aware memory subset, expands it through hub-and-spoke links, and combines it with the recent visual window for frozen-VLM answer prediction. Extensive experiments on both streaming and long video benchmarks show that D-HSM consistently and substantially improves VLM backbones and outperforms other state-of-the-art online and offline video understanding baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Learning to Restore More: Continual Capability Expansion for Pretrained Image Restoration Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30305)
*Hu Gao,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RestoreMore是一个持续能力扩展框架，通过冻结预训练复原模型作为能力锚点，学习残差扩展模块来获取新退化类型的复原能力，并利用双层路由机制实现历史知识和新知识的复用与积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像复原模型以固定能力集训练，当出现新的复原需求时，通常需要额外训练模型或联合重新训练，效率低下且存在灾难性遗忘问题。作者旨在探索如何让已训练好的复原模型持续获取新能力而不遗忘旧能力。

Method: RestoreMore框架包含三个核心设计：(1)冻结预训练复原模型作为能力锚点，保留已有能力；(2)学习残差扩展模块来适配新到达的退化类型；(3)提出能力导向的双层路由机制，第一层识别与当前输入相关的复原能力，第二层选择并组合稀疏的互补退化专家。该机制在多特征阶段运行，使新任务能选择性重用历史复原知识，并逐步丰富后续任务的专家库。

Result: 在广泛的复原基准上的大量实验表明，RestoreMore能够持续获取新的复原能力，同时保持并提升先前学到的能力。

Conclusion: RestoreMore验证了无需重新训练整个模型即可实现图像复原能力的持续扩展，通过冻结锚点与残差扩展模块结合的双层路由机制，实现了新能力的获取和旧能力的保持与提升，为图像复原领域的持续学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Image restoration models are typically trained with a fixed set of capabilities. When new restoration requirements emerge, existing solutions usually train additional models or jointly retrain the original model with both new and historical data. Instead of designing another restoration backbone, we investigate how a trained restorer can continually acquire new capabilities without forgetting those learned previously. We propose RestoreMore, a continual capability-expansion framework that preserves the pretrained restoration model as a frozen capability anchor and learns residual expansion modules for newly arriving degradations. RestoreMore introduces a capability-oriented bi-level routing mechanism at multiple feature stages. The first routing level identifies restoration capabilities relevant to the current input, while the second selects and combines a sparse set of complementary degradation experts. This design enables newly introduced tasks to selectively reuse historical restoration knowledge and progressively enriches the expert bank available for subsequent restoration tasks. Extensive experiments on a wide range of restoration benchmarks demonstrate that RestoreMore consistently acquires new restoration abilities while preserving and improving previously learned capabilities.

</details>


### [182] [ScenePilot: Grow-and-Repair Policy for Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30307)
*Jiawei Zhang,Hongsong Wang,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScenePilot 是一个检索增强的"生长-修复"框架，通过层级检索增强规划（HRAP）模块获取房间级、组级和锚点级布局先验来指导功能组规划，配合强化多模态修复（RMR）模块在每次插入后进行局部修正并在完成后进行全局修复。训练基于 SceneReverse-17k 修复轨迹数据集，该数据集通过对高质量3D场景进行位置、旋转和缩放扰动并反向操作作为修复目标构建。该方法在物理合理性、功能连贯性和可控性上超越一次性生成与全场景优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的3D室内场景生成方法存在三大局限：一次性生成器常产生几何无效布局；事后重优化成本高且不稳定；仅基于提示的规划器缺乏可复用的布局先验来建模功能分组和物体关系。

Method: ScenePilot 采用"生长-修复"两阶段范式：(1) HRAP模块从检索到的房间级、组级、锚点级布局先验中进行功能组规划；(2) 文本驱动的基座生成器顺序插入物体组；(3) RMR模块通过强化学习在每次插入后执行轻量局部修正，并在整个生成完成后进行全局修复。策略从渲染视图、场景状态、检索先验和编辑历史中预测结构化的移动-旋转-缩放动作。训练数据来自SceneReverse-17k，即通过对高质量3D场景施加扰动并反向操作为修复目标构建的轨迹数据集。

Result: ScenePilot 在物理合理性、功能连贯性和可控性方面优于一次性生成和全场景重优化方法，同时保持了生成多样性，并为文本驱动的3D室内场景生成提供了一种更高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过将层级检索先验与强化修复策略相结合，ScenePilot 有效解决了现有方法在布局有效性、优化稳定性和功能先验复用方面的不足，展示了面向3D室内场景生成的高效、可控的增量式生成范式。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D indoor scene generation has advanced from dataset-bound layout modeling to open-vocabulary synthesis with large language and vision-language models. Yet existing methods remain limited: one-pass generators often yield geometrically invalid layouts, heavy post-hoc optimization is costly and unstable, and prompt-only planners lack reusable layout priors for functional grouping and object relations. We propose \textbf{ScenePilot}, a retrieval-augmented \textbf{Grow-and-Repair} framework that formulates scene generation as prior-guided incremental growth with learned rectification. Given a prompt, the Hierarchical Retrieval-Augmented Planning (HRAP) module retrieves room-, group-, and anchor-level layout priors to support functional group planning. A text-driven base generator then inserts object groups sequentially, while the Reinforcement Multimodal Repair (RMR) module performs lightweight local correction after each insertion and a final global repair after completion. To train this policy, we construct \textbf{SceneReverse-17k}, a repair-trajectory dataset built by perturbing high-quality 3D scenes in position, rotation, and scale, then using inverse operations as executable rectification targets. The policy predicts structured \emph{move--rotate--scale} actions from rendered views, scene state, retrieved priors, and edit history. By combining HRAP with RMR, ScenePilot offers an efficient alternative to one-shot generation and heavy full-scene optimization, improving physical plausibility, functional coherence, and controllability while preserving diversity.

</details>


### [183] [Knowing Beyond the Known: Reinforced Knowledge Specification for Multi-Label Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30316)
*Aoting Zhang,Dongbao Yang,Chang Liu,Xiaopeng Hong,Can Ma,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KBK框架，通过显式建模"已知与未知"知识边界来解决多标签类增量学习（MLCIL）中的矛盾，在无需回放缓冲区的条件下超越现有最佳方法2.7%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类增量学习方法在多标签场景下因共现标签和不完整标签导致的学习目标内在矛盾而表现不佳。核心障碍是模型对已知与未知知识的边界模糊，这既损害历史知识保持，又增加当前任务学习难度，并限制对未来概念的适应能力。

Method: 提出KBK（Knowing Beyond the Known）多标签类增量学习框架，包含四个关键组件：（1）层次化特征纯化模块，从全局特征中分离细粒度的类别特定特征，并用低级视觉特征强化高级语义抽象；（2）基于分布先验的不确定性感知回忆增强策略，抑制不可靠预测以提高历史回忆质量；（3）利用语义相关性在共现条件下合成信息丰富的未知特征，为未来学习保留嵌入空间；（4）类别平衡的梯度补偿损失，根据遗忘速度动态重加权梯度反向传播以缓解异构遗忘。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验验证了KBK的有效性和鲁棒性，在MS-COCO B0-C10设定下平均准确率超越此前最佳方法2.7%，且无需任何回放缓冲区。

Conclusion: KBK通过显式区分已知与未知知识并建立统一的学习框架（涵盖历史保持、当前学习和未来适应），有效解决了多标签类增量学习中目标矛盾的问题，证明了无需回放即可实现优异性能的可行性。

Abstract: Existing class-incremental learning methods struggle in multi-label scenarios (MLCIL) due to the inherent contradiction of learning objectives arising from co-occurring and incomplete labels. We argue that the core obstacle is the model's ambiguous boundary between known and unknown knowledge, which undermines historical knowledge retention, complicates current task learning, and limits adaptability to future concepts. To address this, we propose KBK (Knowing Beyond the Known), a reinforced knowledge specification framework that explicitly models what is known or not to unify historical, current, and prospective learning. Specifically, to clarify known knowledge, we develop a hierarchical feature purification module that disentangles fine-grained class-specific features from global features, where high-level semantic abstraction is reinforced with low-level visual features. Additionally, an uncertainty-aware recall enhancement strategy suppresses unreliable predictions based on distribution priors, improving the quality of historical recall. For probing the unknown, KBK leverages semantic correlations to synthesize informative unknown features under co-occurring, preserving embedding space for future learning. Furthermore, to mitigate heterogeneous forgetting, we design a category-balanced gradient compensation loss that dynamically reweights gradient backpropagation according to forgetting speeds. Experiments on multiple benchmarks validate the effectiveness and robustness of KBK, which surpasses prior best methods by 2.7% in Avg. Acc on MS-COCO B0-C10 setting even without any replay buffers.

</details>


### [184] [CapFrame: Text-Instructed Viewpoint Grounding in 3D Gaussian Scenes via Geometric Pseudo Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30342)
*Jirong Li,Satoshi Ikehata,Shuhei Kurita,Ikuro Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出新任务TIVG（文本指导的视点定位），目标是在3D高斯场景中根据文本指令找到与之对齐的6自由度相机位姿。作者提出CapFrame框架，采用Retrieve-Translate-Refine流水线，通过检索相关视图、用MLLM评价排序、将指令翻译为几何伪标签并在3DGS中通过可微优化精修相机位姿。在38个真实场景、135条指令上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS支持照片级实时新视图合成，但放置虚拟相机捕捉期望画面仍主要靠手动操作。已有的语言引导3D场景方法主要关注物体级定位（决定看什么），很少控制单一帧中的呈现方式（如主体朝向或画面布局）。因此需要一种能根据文本指令自动确定相机位姿的方法。

Method: 提出CapFrame框架，包含三步流水线：(1) Retrieve——检索相关视图候选；(2) Translate——通过MLLM的Question-Evaluation过程给候选视图排序，并将指令翻译为几何伪标签（朝向和布局）；(3) Refine——在3DGS中通过可微优化，利用布局损失和朝向损失精修相机位姿。整个框架是部分可微的。

Result: 在38个真实场景、135条指令上进行实验，相比启发式视点搜索和改编的轨迹生成基线，CapFrame生成的视点与文本对齐程度更高，通过VLM指标、MLLM评测和用户研究得到验证。

Conclusion: CapFrame有效解决了3DGS场景中文本指令驱动的视点定位问题，使虚拟相机能够根据自然语言自动捕捉符合语义要求的画面，弥补了现有语言引导方法只关注"看什么"而忽视"怎么看"的空白。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic real-time novel view synthesis, yet placing a virtual camera to capture a desired frame remains largely manual. Existing language-guided approaches in 3D scenes mainly focus on object-centric grounding, determining what to observe but rarely controlling how it should appear in a single frame, such as subject orientation or frame layout. To address this limitation, we introduce a new task, Text-Instructed Viewpoint Grounding (TIVG), which aims to identify a 6-DoF camera pose in a 3D Gaussian scene whose rendered frame aligns with a text instruction. To solve this task, we propose CapFrame, a partially differentiable framework that converts language into geometric pseudo labels for camera pose optimization. CapFrame follows a Retrieve-Translate-Refine pipeline: it retrieves relevant views and ranks them through a Question-Evaluation process with MLLMs, translates the instruction into orientation and layout pseudo labels, and refines the camera pose via differentiable optimization with layout and orientation losses in 3DGS. Experiments on 38 real-world scenes with 135 instructions indicate that CapFrame produces viewpoints better aligned with texts than heuristic viewpoint search and adapted trajectory generation baselines, validated by VLM metrics, MLLM judges, and user studies. Code is available at: https://github.com/jirongli/CapFrame

</details>


### [185] [Proximity3D: Shape from Capacitive Proximity on Sensing Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30344)
*Hao Chen,Chenming Wu,Chun Ping Lam,Xiangjia Chen,Guoxin Fang,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam,Juncong Lin,Chengkai Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于曲面电容织物的形状重建方法，将传感器表面视为非平面感知流形，每个扫描表示为该流形上的电容邻近场，并通过多视图前馈重建模型聚合场信息恢复物体形状。模拟和物理实验证明该方法在曲面传感表面上能稳健重建物体形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数形状重建方法假设测量定义在平面感知域（如RGB图像或深度图）上，缺乏对非平面传感表面的处理能力，制约了机器人通过可穿戴/可变形传感平台实现近场几何感知的可能性。

Method: 使用弯曲的电容织物作为形状传感器，将其表面视为非平面感知流形；每个扫描被表示为该流形上由曲面电极布局与附近物体几何交互诱导的电容邻近场；引入一种多视图前馈重建模型，在已知传感器视角下聚合这些场并恢复观测物体形状。

Result: 通过在模拟和物理实验中的验证，该方法能够从曲面传感表面采集到的电容邻近信号中稳健地重建物体形状。

Conclusion: 这项工作为通过具身感知实现机器人近场几何感知开辟了新途径，证明非平面电容传感平台可作为有效形状感知手段。

Abstract: Most shape reconstruction methods assume measurements defined over planar sensing domains, such as RGB images or depth maps. In this paper, we use a curved capacitive textile as a shape sensor, treating its surface as a non-planar sensing manifold. Each scan is represented as a capacitive proximity field on this manifold, induced by the interaction between the curved electrode layout and nearby object geometry. We introduce a multi-view feedforward reconstruction model that aggregates these fields across known sensor views and recovers the observed object shape. Simulated and physical experiments demonstrate robust reconstruction from capacitive proximity signals acquired on curved sensing surfaces, pointing toward a new route to robotic near-field geometric awareness via embodied sensing.

</details>


### [186] [Seeing the Unseen: Camouflaged Object Detection Beyond the Visible Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30355)
*Avi Gupta,Trasha Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSFormer，一种利用多光谱图像进行伪装目标检测（COD）的端到端框架。与传统依赖三通道RGB图像的方法不同，MSFormer通过捕获更丰富的细粒度光谱信息来提升低可见性场景下的目标检测性能，实验证明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测方法主要依赖传统的三通道RGB图像，其可见光谱范围受限，无法充分利用场景中的细粒度光谱信息。多光谱图像能捕获更广泛的频谱特征，有望为这一复杂的低视觉任务提供更多判别性线索。

Method: 提出端到端框架MSFormer，以多光谱伪装图像作为输入，直接预测对应的二值分割掩码。同时对多光谱波段的整合提供了实证性依据。

Result: 大量实验表明MSFormer方法有效，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 利用多光谱图像进行伪装目标检测是可行且有效的方向，MSFormer证明了多光谱信息在低可见性目标检测任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in camouflaged object detection (COD) have led to substantial progress in challenging low-visibility scenarios, with pioneering studies demonstrating notable success in localizing objects in camouflaged scenes. Despite these achievements, existing approaches predominantly rely on conventional three-channel RGB imagery, thereby constraining the available visual information to a limited spectral range. Multispectral images offer a wide range of information about a scene by capturing fine-grained spectral signatures. Hence, by leveraging multispectral images for COD, we introduce a novel approach to detect camouflaged objects from the corresponding multispectral inputs. In particular, we propose an end-to-end framework, \textbf{\textit{MSFormer}}, that takes a multispectral camouflaged image as input and predicts a binary mask for it. Additionally, we also provide empirical justification for integrating multispectral bands for this complex low-vision task. Our extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, which outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [187] [MCSeg: Pre-training and Fine-tuning Volumetric Pyramid Transformer for Multi-modal Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30371)
*Zhiyu Ye,Hairong Zheng,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MCSeg，一种基于体积Transformer的多模态心脏分割网络，通过创新的Scaling Feature Pyramid桥接单尺度ViT编码器与多尺度CNN解码器，结合掩码图像建模自监督预训练和区域互信息损失，在多个公开数据集上超越11种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络（Transformer+CNN）存在架构不匹配问题，即单尺度的3D ViT编码器输出与多尺度CNN解码器之间无法有效衔接，导致全局上下文信息利用不足，影响心脏分割精度。

Method: 提出MCSeg网络，核心组件为Scaling Feature Pyramid（SFP），该模块将ViT编码器的单尺度输出变换为层次化特征金字塔，有效桥接编码器与解码器。训练采用两阶段范式：先通过掩码图像建模对ViT编码器进行自监督预训练，再在下游任务上微调，并集成区域性互信息（RMI）损失以提升边界分割精度。

Result: 在ImageCHD、MM-WHS、HVSMR-2.0和MSD Heart四个数据集上，MCSeg一致性地超越了11种SOTA方法；在少样本实验中表现优异，展现出对有限数据场景的适应潜力。

Conclusion: MCSeg通过SFP解决了混合架构不匹配问题，并在多模态心脏分割任务中达到SOTA水平，且具备较强的少样本学习能力；代码和预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Automatic cardiac image segmentation is pivotal for diagnosing and treating cardiac diseases. In this work, we introduce MCSeg, a volumetric transformer-based network tailored for multi-modal cardiac segmentation. To overcome the architectural mismatch inherent in existing hybrid networks, we propose a novel Scaling Feature Pyramid (SFP). Unlike conventional skip connections, the SFP effectively bridges the single-scale 3D Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder and the multi-scale CNN decoder by transforming the ViT's output into a hierarchical feature pyramid, ensuring that global contextual information is effectively leveraged. For the training paradigm, the ViT encoder first undergoes self-supervised pre-training via masked image modeling. Subsequently, the network is fine-tuned on downstream tasks, during which a regional mutual information (RMI) loss is integrated to improve boundary segmentation accuracy. In experiments, MCSeg consistently outperforms eleven SOTA methods on CT dataset ImageCHD, multi-modal dataset MM-WHS, MRI dataset HVSMR-2.0 and MSD Heart, highlighting the effectiveness of our MCSeg for multi-modal cardiac segmentation tasks. Furthermore, MCSeg's superior performance in few-shot experiment showcases its significant potential in adapting to limited data scenarios. Codes and pre-trained ViT-B weights are open-sourced at https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenMedIA/MCSeg

</details>


### [188] [PRISM: Predictive Recomposition via Semantic Latent Decomposition for View-invariant Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30388)
*Youngchae Chee,Hosu Lee,Sungjune Park,Junho Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM通过将跨视角视频分解为视图不变和视图可变两个潜在表征，并在语言监督下进行重组，实现了跨视角视频表示学习的解耦，在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角视频表示学习方法将每个视频编码为统一嵌入，导致视图不变和视图可变的语义在共现情况下不可避免地纠缠在一起，即使显式训练视图不变性的跨视角方法也存在此问题，阻碍了动作语义的干净捕获。

Method: PRISM将视频分解为视图不变和视图可变两个潜在表征，并主张一个真正解耦的视图不变特征应当能够与任意视图可变特征充分重组同时保持各自独立语义。基于这一洞察，在语言监督下对两个流进行重组，鼓励两者的干净分解。

Result: 在EgoExo4D、EgoExoLearn和AE2三个基准上取得最先进结果，甚至在零样本设置下超越了领域内（in-domain）模型。

Conclusion: PRISM通过分解与语言监督下的重组策略，有效解决了视图不变与视图可变语义纠缠问题，验证了"可重组性是视图不变特征真正解耦的必要条件"这一核心观点，并在跨视角视频学习中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Cross-view video representation learning aims to capture viewpoint-invariant action semantics despite substantial appearance changes across egocentric and exocentric videos. However, existing methods encode each video as a unified embedding, where view-invariant and view-variant semantics inevitably entangle under co-occurrences - a failure mode we show persists even in cross-view methods explicitly trained for view-invariance. Our key insight is that a view-invariant feature is truly disentangled when it can be sufficiently recomposed with an arbitrary view-variant feature while preserving their independent semantics. Building on this, we propose PRISM, that decomposes video into view-invariant and view-variant latents and recompose them under language supervision encouraging clean decomposition of the two streams. PRISM achieves state-of-the-art results on EgoExo4D, EgoExoLearn, AE2, even surpassing in-domain models under zero-shot setting. Code is available at https://github.com/litcoderr/prism.

</details>


### [189] [Real-Time Scene-Adaptive Tone Mapping for High-Dynamic Range Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30400)
*Gongzhe Li,Linwei Qiu,Peibei Cao,Fengying Xie,Xiangyang Ji,Qilin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自动驾驶场景的HDR图像色调映射方法，通过神经光度校准与尺度不变局部色调映射模型，弥合HDR RAW输入与检测网络LDR sRGB需求之间的差距，实现端到端优化及性能迁移微调，在NVIDIA Jetson平台上可实时处理4K HDR输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数嵌入式神经网络基于LDR图像训练，面对高比特深度HDR图像时因极端动态范围而性能显著下降。传统ISP管线无法有效桥接HDR RAW输入与检测网络所需的LDR sRGB格式之间的域差距。

Method: 提出一种新颖的色调映射方法：一是引入神经光度校准来正则化动态范围；二是采用尺度不变的局部色调映射模型以保留图像细节；三是支持性能迁移微调，使网络能以极小代价从LDR sRGB图像高效适配到HDR RAW图像；整体架构与下游任务进行端到端联合优化。

Result: 在挑战性自动驾驶HDR场景中，该方法优于传统色调映射算法和先进的AI-ISP方法；在NVIDIA Jetson平台上能够对4K高比特深度HDR输入实现实时处理。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了HDR RAW与检测网络LDR sRGB需求之间的差距，兼具高性能与实时性，为嵌入式自动驾驶场景下利用HDR图像增强视觉感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-dynamic-range (HDR) images, with their rich tone and detail reproduction, hold significant potential to enhance computer vision systems, particularly in autonomous driving. However, most neural networks for embedded systems are trained on low-dynamic-range (LDR) inputs and suffer substantial performance degradation when handling high-bit-depth HDR images due to the challenges posed by extreme dynamic ranges. In this paper, we propose a novel tone mapping method that not only bridges the gap between HDR RAW inputs and the LDR sRGB requirements of detection networks but also achieves end-to-end optimization with downstream tasks. Instead of relying on the traditional image signal processing (ISP) pipeline, we introduce neural photometric calibration to regularize dynamic ranges and a scaling-invariant local tone mapping model to preserve image details. In addition, our architecture also supports performance transfer finetuning, enabling efficient adaptation from the LDR sRGB images to the HDR RAW images with minimal cost. The proposed method outperforms traditional tone mapping algorithms and advanced AI-ISP methods in challenging automotive HDR scenes. Moreover, our pipeline achieves real-time processing of 4K high-bit-depth HDR inputs on NVIDIA Jetson platforms.

</details>


### [190] [ImageCAS-X: a dataset and benchmark for coronary artery segmentation and centerline extraction in coronary CT angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30404)
*Kit M. Bransby,Esther Øksnebjerg,Kristoffer Kjær,Jacob Kirkeby,Yasmin El Youssef,Aïda Jiménez,Philip R. Pedersson,Martina C. de Knegt,Klaus F. Kofoed,Rasmus R. Paulsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提供了一个新的冠状动脉管腔分割基准数据集，包含800个来自ImageCAS的CTA扫描的体素级注释（管腔、冠状动脉节段、中心线和网格表面），并按疾病、图像质量、冠脉优势型等临床因素分层评估现有分割方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉管腔的准确分割是CCTA中动脉粥样硬化斑块和血管周围脂肪组织定量评估的前提。虽然已有许多自动化分割方法，但缺乏大规模、高质量、公开可用的标注数据集，导致验证受限。现有方法通常以单一聚合分数报告精度，缺乏在解剖和临床背景下的细致评估。

Method: 基于公开的ImageCAS数据集，构建了包含800个扫描的体素级标注数据集，包括管腔标注、冠脉节段标注、中心线和网格表面。利用该数据集对已建立的管腔分割方法进行基准测试，并与观察者间变异性进行比较，同时按疾病状态、图像质量、冠脉优势型、冠脉节段、血管直径和管腔衰减等维度进行分层性能分析。

Result: 提供了一个大规模高质量的公开基准数据集，实现了对管腔分割方法在解剖和临床背景下的分层性能评估，而不仅仅是单一聚合分数报告。

Conclusion: 该数据集支持管腔分割、斑块和血管周围定量以及血流动力学建模方法的开发与验证，有助于推动CCTA中冠状动脉分割研究的标准化评估。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of the coronary vessel lumen is a prerequisite for quantitative assessment of atherosclerotic plaque and perivascular adipose tissue in coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA). Cardiologists rely on semi-automated methods for this task because manual vessel tracing and segmentation are labour-intensive. Although many automated methods have been proposed, their validation remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality publicly available datasets. We provide a new dataset of voxel-wise annotations of the vessel lumen and coronary segments, alongside centerlines, and mesh surfaces for 800 scans from the publicly available ImageCAS dataset. Using this dataset, we benchmark established lumen segmentation methods against inter-observer variability, stratifying performance by disease, image quality, coronary dominance, coronary segment, vessel diameter, and lumen attenuation. These labels allow segmentation accuracy to be described in anatomical and clinical context rather than reported as a single aggregate score. The dataset supports the development and validation of methods for lumen segmentation, plaque and perivascular quantification, and haemodynamic modelling.

</details>


### [191] [SePArate: Segmenting Patterns from Defects in Wafer Manufacturing Using Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30410)
*Dain Kwon,Changmin Shin,Sunjong Park,Kanghyun Choi,Hyeyoon Lee,Jaewon Jang,Minseok Choi,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SePArate是一种用于半导体晶圆缺陷分割的弱监督方法，仅利用图像级标注即可实现像素级缺陷模式分离，通过三阶段训练（编码器预训练、知识迁移学习空间线索、合成混合缺陷数据训练）在实验中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造中的缺陷分析至关重要，但人工检测无法规模化；现有自动化检测方法在根本原因分析和工艺优化方面仍不够充分，需要更精确的像素级缺陷分割方法。

Method: SePArate采用弱监督学习框架，仅需图像级标注即可实现像素级晶圆缺陷分割。其训练过程分为三个阶段：第一阶段进行编码器预训练；第二阶段进行知识迁移以学习空间线索；第三阶段在合成的混合缺陷数据上进行训练以实现精确分割。

Result: 实验结果表明SePArate在晶圆缺陷分割任务上优于各基线方法，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SePArate通过弱监督方式有效解决了半导体晶圆缺陷像素级分割的难题，在降低标注成本的同时达到了优于基线方法的性能，为半导体制造中的根本原因分析和工艺优化提供了更可靠的自动化支持。

Abstract: In semiconductor manufacturing, defect analysis is essential, but manual inspection cannot scale. However, existing automated inspection methods remain insufficient for root-cause analysis and process optimization. To this end, we present SePArate, a weakly supervised wafer defect segmentation method. SePArate enables pixel-level separation of patterns by leveraging only image-level annotations. It consists of a three-phase training: encoder pretraining, knowledge transfer to learn spatial cues, and training on synthetic mixed-defect data for accurate segmentation. Experiments demonstrate that SePArate outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [192] [Whole-Slide Image Analysis under Realistic Few-Shot Annotation Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30420)
*Tiffanie Godelaine,Maxime Zanella,Karim El Khoury,Benoit Macq,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlideCRF，将条件随机场适配到全切片图像（WSI）的少样本转导（few-shot transduction）场景，结合空间和生物学线索处理类别缺失问题，并设计了符合病理学家真实标注行为（点击和涂鸦）的标注协议。在四个数据集上，SlideCRF在宏F1上显著优于现有转导方法，相比零样本预测提升最高达+37.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本转导方法在全切片图像分析中的评估存在三个不现实的假设：(i) 将多个切片中提取的patch视为独立样本，忽略了复杂组织空间结构；(ii) 数据类别基本平衡，而真实WSI存在严重的类别不平衡且部分类别缺失；(iii) 标注随机采样，无法反映病理学家的真实局部区域标注行为。这些偏差导致现有方法在真实场景中性能不可靠。

Method: 本文包含两项核心贡献：(1) SlideCRF——将条件随机场适配于WSI，通过结合空间邻近性（spatial cues）和生物学组织线索（biological cues）进行联合推理，并为给定切片中可能缺失的类别建模；(2) 提出基于空间局部化点击（clicks）和涂鸦（scribbles）的真实标注协议，模拟病理学家不同类型的交互方式，包括对模型错误的迭代修正。

Result: 在四个数据集上，SlideCRF在宏F1指标上优于现有转导方法。在每个存在的类别提供1个点击时提升零样本预测+24.2%，提供16个点击时提升+37.5%。

Conclusion: 通过将WSI特有的空间结构、生物学先验和类别缺失特性纳入转导推理，并采用贴近真实病理学家行为的标注协议，SlideCRF显著提升了全切片图像零样本分析的精度，为临床数字病理分析提供了更可靠的少样本推理框架。

Abstract: Automating the analysis of whole-slide images has high clinical value, since characterizing cancers requires examining them in detail. Such analysis increasingly relies on vision-language models that provide patch-level zero-shot predictions. However, these predictions remain noisy and must be refined with a few annotations. A promising paradigm for this refinement is few-shot transduction. Rather than treating each patch independently, these methods leverage the relations between patches, together with a few annotations, to refine all predictions jointly. However, current transductive methods are evaluated under conditions that overlook key properties of whole-slide images: (i) datasets consist of independent patches extracted from multiple slides, ignoring the complex tissue organization; (ii) datasets are mostly balanced, whereas a single whole-slide image exhibits severe class imbalance, with several classes absent; and (iii) annotations are sampled at random, without reflecting how a pathologist annotates a limited number of regions. To align the transduction paradigm to realistic whole-slide settings, we introduce the following contributions. First, we propose SlideCRF, which adapts conditional random fields for whole-slide images by combining spatial and biological cues while accounting for classes that may be absent from a given slide. Second, we provide a set of realistic annotation protocols, based on spatially localized clicks and scribbles, modeling different pathologist interactions, such as the iterative correction of model errors. Across four datasets, we show that SlideCRF outperforms current transductive methods in macro F1, improving over the zero-shot predictions by +24.2% and +37.5% with one and 16 clicks per present class, respectively.

</details>


### [193] [ObjectSplat: Improving Mesh Fidelity and Interactivity for 3D Scenes via Object-Level Mesh Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30423)
*Minhas Kamal,Hiranya Garbha Kumar,Mahedi Kamal,Balakrishnan Prabhakaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"先分解后重建"的网格点云（mesh splatting）方法，将场景分解为前景实例和背景分别重建再合成，显著提升网格保真度（F-score提升超5%）和新视角合成质量，并支持物体级别的编辑与交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于splatting的3D重建算法将场景表示为单一的整体场（monolithic field），缺乏物体级结构，难以支持下游编辑与交互；同时未直接观测的区域会被周围纹理污染且无法修正，限制了网格保真度和新视角合成的质量。

Method: 采用"分解-重建"（decompose-before-reconstruct）的流程：首先从每一帧中分割出实例，将剩余部分视为背景并对其进行修复（inpaint），然后分别用mesh splatting独立重建每个实例和背景，最后将各部分合成为一个完整场景。

Result: 该方法在网格保真度上取得了显著提升（F-score提升超过5%），并改善了新视角合成质量，同时支持物体级别的可编辑性和交互性。

Conclusion: 通过将场景先分解为独立实例和背景再分别重建，可以有效克服单体场重建中物体级结构缺失和被遮挡区域纹理污染的问题，在提升重建质量的同时赋予场景物体级的可修改性和交互能力。

Abstract: Splatting-based algorithms reconstruct photorealistic, real-time-renderable, and mesh-exportable 3D scenes from regular images, but they represent a scene as a single monolithic field. Therefore, the reconstruction has no object-level structure, leaving it infeasible for downstream editing or interaction. Moreover, regions that are never directly observed in the input scans are contaminated by the surrounding texture and left uncorrected, capping both mesh fidelity and novel-view synthesis. We propose a decompose-before-reconstruct approach: we segment the instances out of every frame, consider the remaining as background and inpaint it, reconstruct each instance and the background independently with mesh splatting, and compose them into a single scene. Our method significantly improves mesh fidelity (over a 5\% gain in F-score) and novel-view synthesis, while supporting object-wise modifiability and interactivity. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [194] [FlowVVTON: Flow-Guided Mask-Free Video Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30450)
*Shengyao Chen,Xianbing Sun,Liqing Zhang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowVVTON，一个完全mask-free的视频虚拟试穿框架，利用光学流仅在训练时作为监督信号，通过跨所有生成层的flow-warped潜在损失实现多尺度时间一致性，在TikTokDress数据集上大幅超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法依赖人体解析mask或姿态关键点，在大运动和遮挡场景下经常失败，导致边界伪影和时间不一致；且多数方法仅依赖注意力机制进行时间建模，缺乏显式运动监督。

Method: FlowVVTON采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段建立无mask的空间对齐，第二阶段引入flow引导的时间监督。核心创新在于使用光学流作为纯训练时监督信号，在生成模型的所有层上施加flow-warped潜在损失，通过对齐相邻帧特征来强制执行多尺度时间一致性。

Result: 在TikTokDress数据集上，FlowVVTON在时间一致性方面取得显著提升，VFID-R指标相比SwiftTry改善5.7倍，且全程无需分割mask、姿态关键点或区域标注。

Conclusion: FlowVVTON证明了完全消除解析mask依赖的可行性，通过显式物理运动约束的光流监督信号，能同时解决空间对齐和时间一致性问题，为视频虚拟试穿提供了更鲁棒且更简洁的方案。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on aims to transfer a target garment onto a moving person across video frames. Current methods rely on human parsing masks or pose keypoints that frequently fail under large motions and occlusions, causing boundary artifacts and temporal inconsistency. A further limitation is that most approaches rely solely on attention mechanisms for temporal modeling, providing no explicit motion supervision. We propose FlowVVTON, a mask-free framework that eliminates parsing mask dependency entirely. Optical flow is used solely as a training-time supervision signal: a flow-warped latent loss, applied across all layers of the generation model, enforces multi-scale temporal consistency by aligning adjacent-frame features under explicit physical motion constraints. A two-stage training strategy establishes mask-free spatial alignment before introducing flow-guided temporal supervision. Experiments on TikTokDress show that FlowVVTON outperforms baselines by substantial margins, particularly in temporal consistency (5.7$\times$ VFID-R improvement over SwiftTry), while requiring no segmentation masks, pose keypoints, or region annotations at any stage.

</details>


### [195] [SeqAlign3DVG: A Sequence-Aligned Benchmark and Voxel Reasoning Framework for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30451)
*Yi Zhang,Yi Wang,Yueting Wu,Kaiyue Yang,Yuejiao Su,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeqAlign3DVG，一个严格时序对齐且观测对齐的图像级3D视觉定位基准（含9,622个单视图和14,493个序列样本），并提出统一体素流水线（ROVM + PLVF），在无深度协议下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像级3D视觉定位基准存在两个关键缺陷：文本与观测之间的对齐松散，且忽略时序顺序。这限制了具身智能体在真实场景中对三维空间理解的能力评估。

Method: 提出SeqAlign3DVG基准，所有表达式均经过人工验证并严格锚定在提供的RGB观测中（单帧或有序观测序列）。在此基础上提出统一体素流水线，包含两个核心模块：1) Relevance-Ordered Voxel Memory (ROVM)，通过保守记忆动态排序和聚合多视图证据以缓解噪声观测；2) Progressive Language-Voxel Fusion (PLVF)，执行由粗到细的空间-语言推理以实现精确消歧。

Result: 所提方法在无深度（depth-free）协议下达到SOTA性能，显著提升了针对复杂关系和外观线索定义的目标定位精度。

Conclusion: SeqAlign3DVG通过严格的文本-观测对齐和时序排序填补了现有基准空白，所提出的ROVM与PLVF体素流水线为时序多视图3D视觉定位提供了有效方案，验证了保守记忆聚合与渐进融合策略的优越性。

Abstract: Image-based 3D visual grounding is critical for embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks suffer from loose text-observation alignment and neglect temporal ordering. We introduce SeqAlign3DVG, a novel benchmark dedicated to temporally ordered and strictly observation-aligned image-based 3D visual grounding. Unlike prior works using order-agnostic views or global point clouds, SeqAlign3DVG ensures all expressions are human-verified and strictly grounded in the provided RGB observations (single frames or ordered observation sequences). It comprises 9,622 single-view and 14,493 sequence samples featuring rich descriptions, complex relations, and multi-instance ambiguities. To tackle this benchmark, we propose a unified voxel-based pipeline featuring Relevance-Ordered Voxel Memory (ROVM) and Progressive Language-Voxel Fusion (PLVF). ROVM dynamically ranks and aggregates multi-view evidence via a conservative memory to mitigate noisy observations, while PLVF performs coarse-to-fine spatial-linguistic reasoning for precise disambiguation. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance under the depth-free protocol, significantly improving localization for targets defined by complex relations and appearance cues.

</details>


### [196] [Federated Multi-Task Learning for Bladder Tumor Segmentation and MIBC Classification Using a Hybrid CNN-Transformer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30458)
*Malhar Udmale,Divyanshu Dwivedi,Aarohi Dhand,Sachin Dudda Nagaraju,Mayank Rai,Bagesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个联邦多任务学习框架，用于跨四个临床中心的膀胱肿瘤分割和肌层浸润性膀胱癌（MIBC）/非肌层浸润性膀胱癌（NMIBC）分类。采用ResNet-34与Swin-Tiny Transformer结合的Swin Hybrid模型，配合分割引导分类机制和多中心数据增强策略。在FedBCa数据集上取得DSC 0.8100和AUC 0.8931的优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膀胱肿瘤分割和肌层浸润评估对治疗规划至关重要，但跨机构构建稳健模型面临两大挑战：患者数据不能集中存储（隐私限制），以及不同扫描仪和采集协议导致成像特征差异大。现有方法难以在保护隐私的前提下处理多中心数据异构性。

Method: 提出联邦多任务学习框架，同时优化膀胱肿瘤分割和MIBC/NMIBC二分类。模型架构为Swin Hybrid：ResNet-34分支提取局部纹理和边界信息，Swin-Tiny Transformer分支捕捉全局解剖上下文，并通过分割引导分类机制利用肿瘤定位信息辅助MIBC预测。研究还比较了多种数据增强策略在中心化和联邦训练下的表现。

Result: 在FedBCa数据集上，Swin Hybrid在分割和分类之间取得最佳平衡。联邦训练下Geo+Elastic增强组合获得DSC 0.8100和患者级AUC 0.8931，综合得分最高达0.8474。

Conclusion: 研究表明，联邦训练可以在不集中患者数据的情况下，跨多个机构有效执行联合分割和分类任务，为解决医疗领域的隐私约束与多中心模型泛化问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate bladder tumor segmentation and assessment of mus- cle invasion from T2-weighted MRI are important for treatment plan- ning, but developing robust models across institutions is challenging be- cause patient data cannot be centrally pooled and imaging characteristics vary across scanners and acquisition protocols. We propose a federated multi-task learning framework for joint bladder tumor segmentation and MIBC/NMIBC classification across four clinical centers. The proposed Swin Hybrid model combines a ResNet-34 branch for local texture and boundary information with a Swin-Tiny Transformer for global anatomi- cal context. A segmentation-guided classification mechanism further uses tumor localization information to support MIBC prediction. We also investigate several augmentation strategies under both centralized and federated training to improve robustness to multi-center variability. Ex- periments on the FedBCa dataset show that the Swin Hybrid provides the best overall balance between segmentation and classification among the evaluated architectures. Under federated training, Geo+Elastic aug- mentation achieved a DSC of 0.8100 and a patient-level AUC of 0.8931, yielding the highest combined score of 0.8474. These results demonstrate that joint segmentation and classification can be effectively performed across multiple institutions using federated training without centralizing patient data.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Accuracy: Quantifying Pulmonary Attribution in Anatomy-Guided Chest X-Ray Classification Under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30467)
*Abdullah Al Mamun,Md. Nasif Osman Khansur,Md Ashraful Hossen Akash,Md. Kishor Morol,Tze Hui Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DBCA-SegNet-MGAP，一个多任务解剖引导的CNN-Transformer框架，通过双向跨骨干注意力结合互补特征表示并预测软肺掩膜，利用掩膜引导自适应全局平均池化（MGAP）将解剖先验直接纳入分类。在COVID-19数据集上实现加权F1=0.9615、macro ROC-AUC=0.9906；相比常规GAP，MGAP将肺部分配包含率（ALR）从0.3878提升至0.7086、cALR@0.9从0.5265提升至0.9905而分类性能保持不变。外部域迁移下模型保持较高ROC-AUC与肺部分配包含率，但校准性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习胸部X光（CXR）分类模型虽然能达到强分类性能，但无法确认其预测是否主要依赖肺部图像内容。作者旨在超越单纯的诊断性能评估，引入肺部分配包含性（pulmonary attribution containment）这一与解剖相关的可靠性属性，作为独立于诊断性能的新评估维度，并开发能同时提升分类性能与解剖可靠性的框架。

Method: 提出DBCA-SegNet-MGAP框架：(1) 采用CNN-Transformer双骨干架构，通过双向跨骨干注意力融合互补特征；(2) 多任务学习预测软肺掩膜；(3) 通过掩膜引导自适应全局平均池化（MGAP）将解剖先验直接整合进分类流程。评估指标包括解剖局部能量比（ALR）和高强度累积ALR（cALR@0.9）来量化肺部分配包含性，并在三个训练种子上重复实验。

Result: COVID-19四分类内部测试中达到加权F1=0.9615±0.0015、macro ROC-AUC=0.9906±0.0007。架构匹配的双桥对比中，MGAP替代常规GAP使ALR从0.3878提升至0.7086、cALR@0.9从0.5265提升至0.9905，而加权F1基本不变。在锁定Shenzhen-to-Montgomery外部零样本结核病迁移中，ROC-AUC保持0.9080，肺部ALR保持0.6466，但加权F1降至0.7528、ECE升至0.1683。

Conclusion: 研究表明诊断判别能力、校准性能和肺部分配包含性是三个可区分的独立模型属性，应当在内部测试和外部域迁移条件下进行联合评估。MGAP能够在不牺牲分类性能的前提下显著提升模型对肺部区域的注意力集中程度，但外部域转移会导致校准性能下降，提示需要更全面的模型可靠性评估框架。

Abstract: Deep-learning models can achieve strong chest X-ray (CXR) classification performance without establishing whether their predictions predominantly rely on pulmonary image content. This study evaluates pulmonary attribution containment as an anatomy-related reliability property distinct from diagnostic performance. We propose DBCA-SegNet-MGAP, a multi-task anatomy-guided CNN-Transformer framework that combines complementary feature representations through bidirectional cross-backbone attention, predicts a soft lung mask, and incorporates this anatomical prior directly into classification through Mask-Guided Adaptive Global Average Pooling (MGAP). Pulmonary attribution containment is quantified using the Anatomical Local Energy Ratio (ALR) and high-intensity cumulative ALR (cALR@0.9). Experiments were repeated across three training seeds using the COVID-19 Radiography Database for four-class internal testing and a locked Shenzhen-to-Montgomery protocol for zero-shot external tuberculosis testing. On COVID-19, the proposed model achieved a weighted F1 of $0.9615 \pm 0.0015$ and macro ROC-AUC of $0.9906 \pm 0.0007$. In an architecture-matched dual-bridge comparison, replacing conventional GAP with MGAP increased ALR from $0.3878 \pm 0.0098$ to $0.7086 \pm 0.0104$ and cALR@0.9 from $0.5265 \pm 0.0101$ to $0.9905 \pm 0.0018$, while weighted F1 remained essentially unchanged ($0.9618 \pm 0.0015$ vs. $0.9615 \pm 0.0015$). Under locked external transfer to Montgomery, ROC-AUC remained $0.9080 \pm 0.0043$ and pulmonary ALR remained $0.6466 \pm 0.0081$, whereas weighted F1 decreased to $0.7528 \pm 0.0080$ and ECE increased to $0.1683 \pm 0.0055$. These findings show that diagnostic discrimination, calibration, and pulmonary attribution containment are distinct model properties and support their joint evaluation under internal testing and external domain shift.

</details>


### [198] [HorizonNet for visual terrain navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30471)
*Bertil Grelsson,Andreas Robinson,Michael Felsberg,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全景图像和水文地形数据的水面无人艇（USV）定位方法。通过两级CNN架构从360度全景图像中提取地平线，并借助MOSSE相关滤波器将地平线与数字高程模型（DEM）数据在傅里叶域进行匹配，从而获得GPS级精度的位置估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向在沿海区域或群岛环境中作业的水面无人艇（USV），需要可靠且精确的位置估计方法。传统GPS在某些环境下可能受限，因此需要探索替代或补充的定位手段。

Method: 该方法包含三个关键步骤：（1）第一级CNN从360度全景图像中估计近似地平线，并隐式确定相机姿态（俯仰角和横滚角）；（2）根据姿态信息对全景图像进行扭曲校正，生成近似水平相机视角的图像，再由第二级CNN逐像素提取校正后图像中的精确地平线；（3）利用MOSSE相关滤波器在傅里叶域将提取的地平线与DEM数据进行相关性匹配，通过搜索区域内最大相关分数的位置来确定USV的最终位置估计。

Result: 在群岛区域的实地试验中进行了综合实验。实验结果表明，该方法能够实现GPS级精度的位置估计，取得了令人满意的定位性能。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合CNN地平线提取与DEM相关匹配，为USV在近岸和群岛环境中的定位提供了一种有效的视觉解决方案，达到了GPS级精度水平。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of position estimation of unmanned surface vessels (USVs) operating in coastal areas or in the archipelago. We propose a position estimation method where the horizon line is extracted in a 360 degree panoramic image around the USV. We design a CNN architecture to determine an approximate horizon line in the image and implicitly determine the camera orientation (the pitch and roll angles). The panoramic image is warped to compensate for the camera orientation and to generate an image from an approximately level camera. A second CNN architecture is designed to extract the pixelwise horizon line in the warped image. The extracted horizon line is correlated with digital elevation model (DEM) data in the Fourier domain using a MOSSE correlation filter. Finally, we determine the location of the maximum correlation score over the search area to estimate the position of the USV. Comprehensive experiments are performed in a field trial in the archipelago. Our approach provides promising results by achieving position estimates with GPS-level accuracy.

</details>


### [199] [VisER: Visual Evidence and Reliance for Object Hallucination Detection in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30480)
*Afsaneh Hasanebrahimi,Hanxun Huang,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisER，一种训练免费的、双视角的对象级幻觉检测指标，通过分别衡量视觉证据和视觉依赖来区分对象支持来源，在多个LVLM和基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费幻觉检测方法依赖于令牌似然、注意力、视觉置信度或图像-文本相似度等内部信号，但这些信号往往存在来源混淆问题——无法区分对象获得的内部支持是来自对象特定的视觉证据还是生成文本前缀，导致在困难场景下幻觉对象仍可能获得高支持度。

Method: VisER从两个互补视角评估每个生成的对象提及：(1) 视觉证据（Visual Evidence）——衡量对象-上下文兼容性是否由图像令牌中的对象特定证据支持；(2) 视觉依赖（Visual Reliance）——衡量对象是否更多由图像而非生成前缀支持。将两者结合得到更来源感知的接地分数，避免额外的对象级验证生成。

Result: 在多个大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）和多个基准数据集上，VisER在AUROC和AUPR指标上均优于一系列基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过区分内部支持信号的来源（视觉证据 vs 文本前缀），VisER提供了一种更可靠的训练免费对象级幻觉检测方案，有效提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Object hallucination remains a persistent reliability issue in large vision-language models, where generated object mentions may sound plausible but lack visual grounding. Recent training-free detectors use internal signals such as token likelihood, attention, visual confidence, or image-text similarity to identify hallucinated objects. These signals are useful, but they are often source-confounded. They measure how strongly an object is supported inside the model without distinguishing whether that support comes from object-specific visual evidence or the generated text prefix. In difficult cases, a hallucinated object can still receive high internal support because it fits the scene, is associated with nearby visual cues, or follows naturally from the generated text prefix. We propose VisER, a training-free two-sided metric for object-level hallucination detection. VisER evaluates each generated object mention from two complementary views. Visual Evidence measures whether object-context compatibility is backed by object-specific evidence from image tokens. Visual Reliance measures whether the object is supported more by the image than by the generated prefix. Combining these views gives a more source-aware grounding score, while avoiding additional object-level verification generations. Across multiple LVLMs and benchmarks, VisER improves AUROC and AUPR over a range of baselines.

</details>


### [200] [Lot Machine: Multimodal Lot Extraction from Auction Catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30510)
*Mathias Zinnen,Alisha Mund,Sabine Lang,Lukas Hüttner,Thomas Gorges,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化流水线，用于从19至20世纪历史拍卖目录（German Sales数据库）中提取结构化的批次级元数据。系统评估了不同提示策略、约束解码框架及多种部署模式（商业端点、机构网关、本地量化模型），证明在保证输出结构约束的前提下，VLM流水线能够大规模解锁历史拍卖目录的自动分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史拍卖目录是溯源研究和艺术市场研究的关键资源，但其内部格式差异大且缺乏机器可读的批次级表示，严重限制了大规模分析。现有方法无法有效处理这些异构文档，亟需自动化提取结构化元数据的解决方案。

Method: 该研究构建了从German Sales数据库提取拍卖批次元数据的VLM流水线。具体包括：(1) 在人工标注的代表性目录页测试集上评估不同提示策略；(2) 应用约束解码框架以确保输出符合目标结构；(3) 在多种部署模式下进行基准测试，涵盖商业API端点、机构网关（兼顾隐私保护）以及本地托管量化模型，全面反映文化遗产机构的预算、算力和数据隐私约束。

Result: 商业端点在提取性能上达到最高水平；机构网关提供了可行的隐私保护替代方案；本地部署虽然可行，但必须强制执行输出结构约束以保证生成有效的JSON格式。所有部署模式仍不同程度需要人工介入修正，但整体上VLM流水线已能成功支撑大规模自动化分析。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统验证了VLM在不同部署约束下提取历史拍卖目录元数据的可行性和局限性。虽然人工回路修正仍必不可少，但VLM流水线成功解锁了历史拍卖目录的大规模自动化分析，为文化遗产领域的数字人文研究提供了实用路径。

Abstract: For provenance research and art market studies, auction catalogs are an essential resource to trace specific objects over time and space. While historical auction catalogs follow established domain conventions, their internal formatting remains highly variable, and their large-scale analysis is currently restricted by the lack of machine-readable representations of the auction lots. We propose a pipeline to automatically extract structured lot-level metadata from German Sales, a large database of historical auction and sales catalogs from the 19th and 20th centuries. Using a manually annotated test set of representative catalog pages, we evaluate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under varying prompt strategies and constrained decoding frameworks. To reflect the practical constraints faced by cultural heritage institutions, including budget, compute resources, and data privacy requirements, we benchmark the methods across different deployment modes ranging from commercial providers to locally hosted, quantized models. We find that commercial endpoints establish the performance ceiling, while institutional gateways offer a viable, privacy-preserving alternative. Local deployments remain feasible, but strictly require enforcing the output structure during generation to guarantee a valid JSON format. While varying degrees of human-in-the-loop correction are still necessary, this work demonstrates that a VLM-based pipeline can successfully unlock historical auction catalogs for large-scale automated analysis.

</details>


### [201] [MEOM: Multi-View Expected-OKS Maximization for Human Pose Triangulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30521)
*Ziliang Xiong,Henglin Shi,Per-Erik Forssen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用完整热图进行多视角3D人体姿态估计的新方法。通过引入多视角期望OKS最大化（MEOM）目标函数来定位3D关节点，并采用最高密度区域（HDR）校准作为热图可靠性的诊断方法。该方法适用于有无3D监督两种设置，在无监督设置下与依赖更大骨干网络、时序融合及模拟3D数据的方法性能相当；在有监督设置下，在Human3.6M上达到19.11mm绝对MPJPE，并以一半推理成本超越最先进体积方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代数三角化方法中，从预测热图解码2D关键点存在固有缺陷：热图在遮挡下可能是多模态的，将其压缩为单一峰值会丢失空间分布信息。因此需要利用完整热图来更准确地估计3D姿态，这需要解决两个核心问题：如何在不同视角间鲁棒地融合热图，以及如何评估热图的可靠性。

Method: 提出MEOM（Multi-view Expected-OKS Maximization）目标函数，通过最大化各视角在概率质量上的一致性来定位3D关节点。同时采用最高密度区域（HDR）校准作为热图质量/可靠性的诊断指标，独立于基于距离的度量。框架覆盖两种设置：无3D监督时，通过最大化MEOM从预训练热图预测器优化3D姿态；有3D监督时，使用MEOM与MSE损失的组合进行端到端训练。

Result: 在无3D监督设置下，与依赖更大骨干网络、时序融合和模拟3D数据的SOTA方法性能相当，且在模糊的Human3.6M（H36MA）和遮挡的CMU Panoptic帧上优势显著。在有3D监督设置下，在Human3.6M上达到19.11mm绝对MPJPE，优于SOTA体积方法，且推理成本仅为后者的一半。

Conclusion: 利用完整热图而非单一峰值估计3D姿态是更优的策略。MEOM目标函数结合HDR校准有效解决了热图跨视角融合和可靠性评估问题，在两种监督设置下均展现了先进的性能与效率优势。

Abstract: Conventional algebraic triangulation solves 3D human pose estimation (HPE) from multi-view 2D keypoints. The typical approach, decoding 2D keypoints from predicted heatmaps, is unreliable as heatmaps can be multimodal under occlusion, and collapsing them into single peaks discards their spatial distribution. We seek to use the entire heatmap to estimate 3D poses more accurately, which requires solving two problems: how to robustly fuse heatmaps across views, and how to assess the reliability of heatmaps. For the former, we introduce a novel objective, Multi-viewExpected-OKS Maximization (MEOM), that locates a 3D joint where the views agree in probability mass. For the latter, we adopt highest-density-region (HDR) calibration as a diagnostic of that mass, independently of distance-based metrics. The proposed framework covers two settings, with and without 3D supervision. Without 3D supervision, we optimize 3D poses from pretrained heatmap predictors by maximizing MEOM, achieving comparable performance with state-of-the-art methods that rely on larger backbones, temporal fusion, and simulated 3D data. On ambiguous Human3.6M (H36MA) and occluded CMU Panoptic frames, the advantage is substantial. When 3D labels are available, we train the model end-to-end with a combined MEOM and MSE loss, achieving 19.11 mm absolute MPJPE on Human3.6M outperforming the state-of-the-art volumetric approach on absolute MPJPE at half the inference cost.

</details>


### [202] [Minerals in the Wild: A Hyperspectral-XRF Dataset for Elemental Composition Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30537)
*Eleftheria Tetoula-Tsonga,George Arvanitakis,Theodoros Giannakas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个名为"Minerals in the Wild"的多用途矿物高光谱数据集，包含1,132块来自欧洲的岩石标本，每块标本配有HSI高光谱采集和XRF元素表征。作者提出了一种基于凸优化的HSI像素与USGS光谱特征匹配方法，通过剪枝机制移除不相关的USGS字典条目，实验表明该方法优于简单基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速矿物表征对矿产勘探和工业矿石加工至关重要。高光谱成像（HSI）因其细粒度光谱分辨率在矿物判别中具有潜力，但缺乏带有可靠地面真值标签的公开数据集，阻碍了基于HSI的矿物识别方法的发展与评估。

Method: （1）构建Minerals in the Wild数据集，包含1,132块欧洲岩石标本的HSI采集及XRF元素表征；（2）定义元素表征任务；（3）提出剪枝机制，在凸优化匹配HSI像素与USGS光谱签名之前，移除USGS字典中距离较远的签名。

Result: 实验表明所提出的剪枝+凸优化匹配方法在元素表征任务上优于简单基线方法。

Conclusion: 该数据集为HSI矿物识别研究提供了带可靠标注的大规模公共资源，所提出的匹配方法验证了结合字典剪枝与凸优化进行矿物光谱匹配的有效性。

Abstract: Rapid mineral characterization is essential for applications ranging from mineral exploration to industrial ore processing. To this end, Hyperspectral Imaging (HSI) has emerged as a promising sensing modality thanks to its fine spectral resolution, enabling mineral discrimination in both close-range and remote sensing settings. However, the scarcity of publicly available datasets with reliable ground-truth labels hinders the development and evaluation of HSI-based mineral identification methods. We release Minerals in the Wild, a multi-purpose dataset comprising 1,132 rock specimens collected across Europe. For each specimen, we provide an HSI acquisition together with an elemental characterization obtained via an XRF sensor. We define the task of elemental characterization on our dataset and propose a pruning mechanism that removes distant signatures from the USGS dictionary prior to a convex optimization approach for matching HSI pixels with USGS spectral signatures. Finally, we empirically show that our approach outperforms simpler baselines.

</details>


### [203] [Modality Disentangled Learning for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Recognition: A Primitive Memory Distillation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30563)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Zheng Pang,Mengting Li,Yiqi Wang,Guangyuan Dong,Chao Xue,Yusen Wu,Zihao Li,Huy Phan,Sicheng Zhao,Björn W. Schuller,Jiachen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Primitive Memory Distillation (PriMD)框架，从模态内部视角解决多模态情感识别中的模态缺失问题。PriMD将跨模态共享语义与模态特定表示分离，将后者离散化为可学习的语义原语构建模态特定记忆库。当模态缺失时，学生模型利用可用模态的共享语义作为查询动态检索原语，在受限记忆空间中补偿缺失的模态特定信息，并与教师模型对齐。在IEMOCAP、CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上取得SOTA性能，并在多种缺失模态设置下展现出更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MER方法在缺失模态场景下通常将缺失模态作为一个整体进行生成、对齐或蒸馏，忽视了各模态内部所承载信息的异质性。这种整体性处理将可推断的共享语义与不确定的模态特定细节混在一起，导致表示不稳定并降低模型鲁棒性。

Method: PriMD框架采用模态内视角：(1) 首先将跨模态共享语义与模态特定表示进行解耦；(2) 将模态特定表示离散化为可学习的语义原语，构建模态特定记忆库；(3) 在模态缺失时采用教师-学生框架，学生模型以可用模态的共享语义为查询，动态检索原语，在受限记忆空间中补偿缺失的模态特定信息，并与教师模型对齐。

Result: 在IEMOCAP、CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PriMD在多种缺失模态设置下均取得最先进性能，表现出持续更强的鲁棒性，同时缓解了整体特征推断所导致的不稳定性。

Conclusion: PriMD通过将模态特定信息离散化为语义原语并在受限记忆空间中进行检索补偿，有效解决了MER中缺失模态时整体式特征推断带来的不稳定问题，为多模态情感识别在真实场景下的鲁棒性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition (MER) systems often suffer from missing modalities in real-world scenarios. Existing methods usually generate, align, or distill missing modalities as a whole, overlooking the heterogeneous nature of the information carried by each modality. Such holistic treatment mixes inferable shared semantics with uncertain modality-specific details, yielding unstable representations and degrading robustness. To address this issue, we propose the Primitive Memory Distillation (PriMD) framework. Unlike existing methods, PriMD takes an intra-modal perspective and focuses on how different types of information within a modality differ in recoverability within each modality. PriMD first disentangles cross-modal shared semantics from modality-specific representations, and then discretizes the latter into learnable semantic primitives to construct modality-specific memory banks. When modalities are missing, PriMD is a teacher-student framework that the student model uses the shared semantics of available modalities as queries to dynamically retrieve primitives. It compensates for missing modality-specific information within a constrained memory space and aligns with the teacher model. Extensive experiments on IEMOCAP, CMU-MOSI, and CMU-MOSEI demonstrate that PriMD achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently stronger robustness across a wide range of missing-modality settings, while mitigating the instability caused by holistic feature inference. Our code and project website are available at https://github.com/JiaqiZhang-Sengoku/PriMD and https://jiaqizhang-sengoku.github.io/PriMD/, respectively.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Compositional Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding with Synthetic Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30584)
*Xingjian Wang,Shijian Wang,Yibo Wang,Zihao Yu,Runhao Fu,Xuelian Cheng,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合时空视频定位（CompSTVG）任务，针对现有STVG方法在处理复杂组合查询（需同时推理属性和关系）时的不足。作者构建了基于时空场景图的合成数据引擎，生成难度分级的STVG-CompBench基准，并通过课程强化学习框架CurrSTVG在最具挑战性的组合查询上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STVG的评估与训练数据主要聚焦于简单查询，忽视了真实场景中普遍存在的组合查询——目标需要通过其属性及其与其他实体关系的联合推理来消歧。这导致现有模型在组合场景下性能大幅下降。

Method: 1) 定义CompSTVG任务，要求模型处理交织属性与关系线索的组合查询；2) 构建合成数据引擎，以时空场景图作为难度度量，将难度可控的查询合成建模为约束规划问题，生成难度分级数据；3) 构建STVG-CompBench基准，按显式难度层次分层，同时涵盖时间复杂度和空间干扰；4) 提出CurrSTVG课程强化学习框架用于训练。

Result: 在STVG-CompBench上评估11个代表性STVG模型，发现现有模型在组合查询上表现不佳，性能急剧下降，且这种下降通常被整体数据集平均指标所掩盖。CurrSTVG框架带来一致增益，在最具挑战性的组合查询上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 当前STVG模型在组合查询上存在明显短板，需要更复杂的数据基准和训练方法来应对。CurrSTVG验证了课程强化学习在提升组合时空定位能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Despite the impressive progress of recent MLLMs on spatio-temporal video grounding (STVG), existing evaluations and training data focus primarily on simple queries. They largely overlook the compositional queries prevalent in real-world scenarios, where a target must be disambiguated by jointly reasoning about its attributes and relations to other entities. To bridge this gap, we propose Compositional Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (CompSTVG), a task that requires models to process complex textual queries where every intertwined attribute and relational cue is essential for disambiguation. To facilitate this task at scale, we build a synthetic data engine that leverages a spatio-temporal scene graph as a difficulty measure and casts difficulty-controlled query synthesis as a constraint programming problem, producing difficulty-graded data for both evaluation and training. Built on this engine, we introduce STVG-CompBench, a benchmark stratified by explicit difficulty levels that jointly capture temporal complexity and spatial interference.
